\displaystyle=:\sum_{k=17}^{24}I_{k}.

After substituting the following equation from applying horizontal derivative to
\eqref{eq:dh-FBCPE}_{2}
,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}Z_{hh}+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{hh}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}Z_{hh}

\displaystyle~~~~+2(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{h}+2\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}}Z_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~+\alpha\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{hh}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,(Zv_{hh})}=0,

one has that

\displaystyle-g\int z^{\alpha}Z_{hh}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,(Zv_{hh})\,d\vec{x}=g\int Z_{hh}\bigl(\partial_{t}Z_{hh}+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{hh}

\displaystyle~~~~+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}Z_{hh}+2(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{h}+2\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}}Z_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~+\alpha\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{hh}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z\bigr)\,d\vec{x}=\dfrac{d}{dt}\biggl\{\dfrac{g}{2}\int\bigl|Z_{hh}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}\biggr\}

\displaystyle~~~~+g\int Z_{hh}\bigl(\dfrac{1-\alpha}{2}\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}Z_{hh}+2(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~+2\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}}Z_{h}+\alpha\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{hh}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z\bigr)\,d\vec{x}=:\dfrac{d}{dt}\biggl\{\dfrac{g}{2}\int\bigl|Z_{hh}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}\biggr\}-I_{25}.

Now we estimate
I_{17},\cdots,I_{25}
. Substituting
\eqref{eq:dh-FBCPE}_{3}
in
I_{19},I_{22}
yields

\displaystyle I_{19}=(\alpha+1)\int\biggl[Z^{\alpha}\bigl(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}v}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v\,d\xi\bigr)\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\partial_{z}v\cdot(Zv_{hhh}+Z_{h}v_{hh})\biggr]\,d\vec{x}+(\alpha+1)\int\biggl[Z^{\alpha}\bigl(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}

\displaystyle~~~~-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v_{h}\,d\xi\bigr)\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z\partial_{z}v\cdot(Zv_{hhh}+Z_{h}v_{hh})\biggr]\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\int\biggl[Z^{\alpha}\bigl(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}\,d\xi\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\partial_{z}v\cdot(Zv_{hhh}+Z_{h}v_{hh})\biggr]\,d\vec{x}=:I_{19}^{\prime}+I_{19}^{\prime\prime}+I_{19}^{\prime\prime\prime},

\displaystyle I_{22}=-(\alpha+1)\int\biggl[Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}v}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v\,d\xi\bigr)\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\partial_{z}v_{h}\cdot v_{hh}\biggr]\,d\vec{x}-(\alpha+1)\int\biggl[Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}

\displaystyle~~~~-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v_{h}\,d\xi\bigr)\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z\partial_{z}v_{h}\cdot v_{hh}\biggr]\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-\int Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}\,d\xi\bigr)\partial_{z}v_{h}\cdot v_{hh}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~=:I_{22}^{\prime}+I_{22}^{\prime\prime}+I_{22}^{\prime\prime\prime}.

Also, substituting (
1.18
) in
I_{20},I_{23}
yields,

\displaystyle I_{20}=\alpha(\alpha+1)\int\biggl[Z^{\alpha-2}Z_{h}\bigl(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}v}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v\,d\xi\bigr)\cdot\nabla_{h}Z

\displaystyle~~~~\times\partial_{z}v\cdot(Zv_{hhh}+Z_{h}v_{hh})\biggr]\,d\vec{x}+\alpha\int\biggl[Z^{\alpha-1}Z_{h}\bigl(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}

\displaystyle~~~~-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\,d\xi\bigr)\partial_{z}v\cdot(Zv_{hhh}+Z_{h}v_{hh})\biggr]\,d\vec{x}=:I_{20}^{\prime}+I_{20}^{\prime\prime},

\displaystyle I_{23}=-\alpha(\alpha+1)\int\biggl[Z^{\alpha-1}Z_{h}\bigl(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}v}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v\,d\xi\bigr)\cdot\nabla_{h}Z

\displaystyle~~~~\times\partial_{z}v_{h}\cdot v_{hh}\biggl]\,d\vec{x}-\alpha\int\biggl[Z^{\alpha}Z_{h}\bigl(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}

\displaystyle~~~~-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\,d\xi\bigr)\partial_{z}v_{h}\cdot v_{hh}\biggr]\,d\vec{x}=:I_{23}^{\prime}+I_{23}^{\prime\prime}.

Then after applying Hölder’s, Minkowski’s, and the Sobolev embedding inequalities, we have

\displaystyle I_{19}^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\biggl[z^{\alpha/2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl(\bigl|v_{hhh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|v_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigr)\bigl(\bigl|Z_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl(\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}+(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}\bigr)\biggr]\,dz\lesssim\bigl(1+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}},

\displaystyle I_{19}^{\prime\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\biggl[z^{\alpha/2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{8}}\bigl(\bigl|v_{hhh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|v_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigr)\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl(\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}\bigr|_{L^{8}}+(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{8}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}\bigr)\biggr]\,dz\lesssim\bigl(1+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}},

\displaystyle I_{19}^{\prime\prime\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\biggl[z^{\alpha/2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl(\bigl|v_{hhh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|v_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigr)\bigl[\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}^{2}v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}\bigr]\biggr]\,dz\lesssim\bigl(1+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}},

\displaystyle I_{22}^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\biggl[z^{\alpha/2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|v_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl(\bigl|Z_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl(\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\,d\xi)^{1/2}\bigr)\biggr]\,dz\lesssim\bigl(1+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{3/2}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}},

\displaystyle I_{22}^{\prime\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\biggl[z^{\alpha/2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|v_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl(\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}\bigr|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}\bigr)\biggr]\,dz\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{3/2}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}},

\displaystyle I_{22}^{\prime\prime\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}z^{\alpha/2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|v_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl[\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}^{2}v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}+(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}\bigr]\,dz

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{3/2}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}},

\displaystyle I_{20}^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\biggl[z^{\alpha/2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl(\bigl|v_{hhh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|v_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigr)\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{8}}^{2}\bigl[\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}\bigr]\biggr]\,dz\lesssim\bigl(1+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{1/2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}},

\displaystyle I_{20}^{\prime\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\biggl[z^{\alpha/2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl(\bigl|v_{hhh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|v_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigr)\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{8}}\bigl[\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}v}\bigr|_{L^{8}}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{8}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}\bigr]\biggr]\,dz\lesssim\bigl(1+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{1/2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}},

\displaystyle I_{23}^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\biggl[z^{\alpha/2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|v_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\bigl[\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}\bigr]\biggr]\,dz\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{3/2}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{1/2}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}},

\displaystyle I_{23}^{\prime\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\biggl[z^{\alpha/2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|v_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl[\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}v}\bigr|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}\bigr]\biggr]\,dz\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{3/2}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{1/2}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}.

We list the estimates for the rest in the following:

\displaystyle I_{17}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\biggl[\bigl(\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl(\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{3}v\bigr|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigr)\biggr]\,dz\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+1\bigl)^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{18}\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigl\Arrowvert v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert v_{hhh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert v_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl(1+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}},

\displaystyle I_{21}\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{t}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert v_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert v_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}},

\displaystyle I_{24}\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert Z-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert v_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigr)\lesssim\bigl(1+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert Z-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}},

\displaystyle I_{25}\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\overline{z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{4}}\bigl\Arrowvert\overline{z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}^{2}v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{4}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}},

where we have applied (
3.16
). Then summing up the above inequalities from (
3.27
) yields

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\biggl\{\dfrac{1}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|v_{hh}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{g}{2}\int\bigl|Z_{hh}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}\biggr\}

(3.28)

\displaystyle\int Z\bigl(\mu\bigl|\nabla_{h}v_{hh}\bigr|^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bigl|\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{hh}\bigr|^{2}+\mu\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{hh}\bigr|^{2}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{H}(\bigl\Arrowvert Z-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}},\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}},\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}})\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr).

Integrating this inequality in the temporal variable implies (
3.24
).
∎

3.3
The spatial derivative estimates

In this section, we will present some spatial derivative estimates for
Z
and
v
. The main goal is to recover the dissipation factor of
Z
and the estimates for the vertical (normal) derivatives (i.e.,
\partial_{z}
) of
v
.

We start by studying the dissipation of
\nabla_{h}Z
. Indeed, since
Z
is independent of the
z
-variable, by making use of the boundary conditions in (
1.20
), integrating
\eqref{FB-CPE}_{2}
in the vertical direction yields the identity,

\displaystyle\dfrac{g}{\alpha+1}Z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}Z=\mu\overline{\Delta_{h}v}+(\mu+\lambda)\overline{\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\nabla_{h}v}+\mu\nabla_{h}\log Z\cdot\overline{\nabla_{h}v}

(3.29)

\displaystyle+(\mu+\lambda)\overline{\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}\nabla_{h}\log Z-Z^{\alpha}\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{t}}-Z^{\alpha}\overline{z^{\alpha}v\cdot\nabla_{h}v}

\displaystyle+Z^{\alpha}\overline{\partial_{z}(z^{\alpha}W)v}.

Meanwhile, from (
1.18
), one has

\displaystyle\partial_{z}(z^{\alpha}W)=(\alpha+1)z^{\alpha}\bigl((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}\bigr)

(3.30)

\displaystyle-(\alpha+1)z^{\alpha}v\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z-z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v.

Thus, after plugging this identity into the terms in (
3.29
), applying Hölder’s inequality and the Sobolev embedding inequalities leads to the following estimates:

\displaystyle\bigl|Z^{\alpha}\overline{\partial_{z}(z^{\alpha}W)v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl(\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{2}\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr|_{\infty}^{2}\,dz+\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{2}}\,dz

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\bigl|Z^{\alpha}\overline{z^{\alpha}v\cdot\nabla_{h}v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{2}}\,dz

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\bigl|Z^{\alpha}\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{t}}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\bigl|\mu\overline{\Delta_{h}v}+(\mu+\lambda)\overline{\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\nabla_{h}v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\bigl|\mu\nabla_{h}\log Z\cdot\overline{\nabla_{h}v}+(\mu+\lambda)\overline{\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}\nabla_{h}\log Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}.

Therefore we have from (
3.29
) that

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}=\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

(3.31)

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}.

With (
3.31
) in hand, we will be able to derive the dissipation of
\partial_{zz}v
. In fact, after taking inner product of
\eqref{FB-CPE}_{2}
with
-Z\partial_{zz}v
, applying integration by parts in the resultant equation yields,

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\biggl\{\dfrac{1}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|v_{z}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}\biggr\}+\int Z\bigl(\mu\bigl|\nabla_{h}\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bigl|\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}

(3.32)

\displaystyle+\mu\bigl|\partial_{zz}v\bigr|^{2}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}=g\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\nabla_{h}Z\cdot\partial_{zz}v\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle-\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}v_{z}\cdot\nabla_{h}v\cdot\partial_{z}v\,d\vec{x}-\int Z^{\alpha+1}(z^{\alpha}W)_{z}\partial_{z}v\cdot\partial_{z}v\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle-\alpha\int z^{\alpha-1}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl(\partial_{t}v+v\cdot\nabla_{h}v)\cdot\partial_{z}v\,d\vec{x}=:\sum_{k=26}^{29}I_{k}.

Applying Young’s inequality implies that, for any constant
\delta>0
,

I_{26}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta^{-1}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Also, after substituting (
3.30
), we have

\displaystyle I_{28}=-(\alpha+1)\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}\bigr)\partial_{z}v\cdot\partial_{z}v\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-\int Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl((\alpha+1)z^{\alpha}v\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z+z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\bigr)\partial_{z}v\cdot\partial_{z}v\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\bigl(\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\bigr)\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\,dz+\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}.

Applying Hölder’s, Hardy’s (Lemma
2
), and the Sobolev embedding inequalities, yields,

\displaystyle I_{29}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{-1}\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{-1}\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta^{-1}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}},

where we have used (
3.16
) and the following inequalities from Lemma
2
,

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert z^{-1}\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}=\int_{0}^{1}z^{-2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v-\partial_{z}v|_{z=0}\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dz

(3.33)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|\partial_{zz}v\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dz=\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

since
\partial_{z}v\big|_{z=0,1}=0
from (
1.20
).
Similarly, we list the rest of the estimates in the following:

\displaystyle I_{27}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}.

Thus, from (
3.31
), (
3.32
) and the above inequalities with suitably small
\delta
, we have the following inequality:

\displaystyle\int\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

(3.34)

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{4}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2},

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\biggl\{\dfrac{1}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|v_{z}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}\biggr\}+\int Z\bigl(\mu\bigl|\nabla_{h}\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bigl|\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}

\displaystyle+\mu/2\bigl|\partial_{zz}v\bigr|^{2}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\int\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{H}(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}},\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}},\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}})\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigr).

Then integrating the above inequalities in the temporal variable concludes the following proposition:

Proposition 8
.

Under the same assumptions as in Proposition
4
, one has that

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|v_{z}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\int_{0}^{T}\int\biggl(Z\bigl(\mu\bigl|\nabla_{h}\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bigl|\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}

(3.35)

\displaystyle+\mu/2\bigl|\partial_{zz}v\bigr|^{2}\bigr)+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|^{2}\biggr)\,d\vec{x}\,dt\leq\dfrac{1}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z_{0}^{\alpha+1}\bigl|v_{0,z}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{T}\biggl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\biggr)\,dt+C\int_{0}^{T}\biggl(\mathcal{H}(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}},

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}},\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}})\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigr)\biggr)\,dt,

for some constant
0<C<\infty
.

We will perform similar analysis to
\eqref{eq:dh-FBCPE}_{1}
in the following. The result leads to the following proposition:

Proposition 9
.

Under the same assumptions as in Lemma
4
, one has that

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|v_{hz}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\int_{0}^{T}\int Z\bigl(\mu\bigl|\nabla_{h}\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr|^{2}

(3.36)

\displaystyle+(\mu+\lambda)\bigl|\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr|^{2}+\mu/2\bigl|\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr|^{2}\bigr)+\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}\,dt

\displaystyle\lesssim\dfrac{1}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z_{0}^{\alpha+1}\bigl|v_{0,hz}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{3}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dt

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{T}\mathcal{H}(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}},\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}},\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}})

\displaystyle\times\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr)\,dt.

Proof.

Applying
\partial_{h}
to (
3.29
) and (
3.30
) gives us the following identities:

\displaystyle\dfrac{g}{\alpha+1}Z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}Z_{h}=-\dfrac{\alpha g}{\alpha+1}Z^{\alpha-1}Z_{h}\nabla_{h}Z+\mu\overline{\Delta_{h}v_{h}}+(\mu+\lambda)\overline{\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\nabla_{h}v_{h}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\mu\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}\cdot\overline{\nabla_{h}v}+(\mu+\lambda)\overline{\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}+\mu\nabla_{h}\log Z\cdot\overline{\nabla_{h}v_{h}}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\mu+\lambda)\overline{\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}}\nabla_{h}\log Z-Z^{\alpha}\overline{z^{\alpha}\partial_{t}v_{h}}-Z^{\alpha}\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}\cdot\nabla_{h}v}

\displaystyle~~~~-Z^{\alpha}\overline{z^{\alpha}v\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{h}}+Z^{\alpha}\overline{\partial_{z}(z^{\alpha}W)v_{h}}+Z^{\alpha}\overline{\partial_{z}(z^{\alpha}W_{h})v}-\alpha Z^{\alpha-1}Z_{h}\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{t}}

\displaystyle~~~~-\alpha Z^{\alpha-1}Z_{h}\overline{z^{\alpha}v\cdot\nabla_{h}v}+\alpha Z^{\alpha-1}Z_{h}\overline{\partial_{z}(z^{\alpha}W)v},

(3.37)

and

\displaystyle\partial_{z}(z^{\alpha}W_{h})=(\alpha+1)z^{\alpha}\bigl((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}}\bigr)-(\alpha+1)z^{\alpha}v_{h}\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z-(\alpha+1)z^{\alpha}v\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~-z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}.

(3.38)

Now we estimate the
L^{2}
-norm of
\nabla_{h}^{2}Z
. It suffices to calculate the
L^{2}
norms of the right-hand side of (
3.37
). Inserting (
3.38
) in the following and applying Minkowski’s inequality yield,

\displaystyle\bigl|Z^{\alpha}\overline{\partial_{z}(z^{\alpha}W_{h})v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl[\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}+\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}^{2}v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\bigr]+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\,dz

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl(\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\bigr)\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\,dz+\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr|_{L^{2}}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\,dz

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl(1+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}.

Similarly, substituting (
3.30
) into the terms involving
\partial_{z}(z^{\alpha}W)
implies,

\displaystyle\bigl|Z^{\alpha}\overline{\partial_{z}(z^{\alpha}W)v_{h}}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl(\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}+\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}v}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\,dz+\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\,dz

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl(1+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}},

\displaystyle\bigl|Z^{\alpha-1}Z_{h}\overline{\partial_{z}(z^{\alpha}W)v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl(\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}v}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigr)+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\,dz+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\,dz\lesssim\bigl(1+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}.

We list the rest of estimates in the following:

\displaystyle\bigl|Z^{\alpha-1}Z_{h}\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2},

\displaystyle\bigl|Z^{\alpha}\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}\cdot\nabla_{h}v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\,dz\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2},

\displaystyle\bigl|Z^{\alpha}\overline{z^{\alpha}v\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{h}}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr|_{L^{2}}\,dz

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}},

\displaystyle\bigl|Z^{\alpha-1}Z_{h}\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{t}}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|\overline{v_{t}}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\lesssim\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{H^{1}}\bigl|\overline{v_{t}}\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\bigl|\overline{v_{t}}\bigr|_{H^{1}}^{1/2}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigr),

\displaystyle\bigl|Z^{\alpha-1}Z_{h}\overline{z^{\alpha}v\cdot\nabla_{h}v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}\,dz

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}},

\displaystyle\bigl|Z^{\alpha}\overline{z^{\alpha}\partial_{t}v_{h}}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\bigl|\mu\overline{\Delta_{h}v_{h}}+(\mu+\lambda)\overline{\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\nabla_{h}v_{h}}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{3}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\bigl|\mu\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}\cdot\overline{\nabla_{h}v}+(\mu+\lambda)\overline{\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}+\mu\nabla_{h}\log Z\cdot\overline{\nabla_{h}v_{h}}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\mu+\lambda)\overline{\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}}\nabla_{h}\log Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}},

where we have applied (
3.16
). Then from (
3.37
), we have

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}=\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{3}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

(3.39)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{H}(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}},\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}})\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigr).

Integrating the square of (
3.39
) in the temporal variable yields,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dt\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{3}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

(3.40)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{H}(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}},\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}})\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}\bigr)\,dt.

Next, we will derive the dissipation of
\nabla_{h}\partial_{zz}v
. After taking inner product of
\eqref{eq:dh-FBCPE}_{1}
with
-Z\partial_{zz}v_{h}
and applying integration by parts, we have the following equation:

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\biggl\{\dfrac{1}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|v_{hz}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}\biggr\}+\int Z\bigl(\mu\bigl|\nabla_{h}\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr|^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bigl|\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr|^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\mu\bigl|\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr|^{2}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}=\int\mu\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{z}\cdot Z\partial_{z}v_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{z}\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}\cdot Z\partial_{z}v_{h}\,d\vec{x}+g\int(z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}Z)_{h}\cdot Z\partial_{zz}v_{h}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}v_{z}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{h}\cdot\partial_{z}v_{h}\,d\vec{x}-\int Z^{\alpha+1}(z^{\alpha}W)_{z}\partial_{z}v_{h}\cdot\partial_{z}v_{h}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-\alpha\int z^{\alpha-1}Z^{\alpha+1}(\partial_{t}v_{h}+v\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{h})\cdot\partial_{z}v_{h}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}v_{h}\cdot\nabla_{h}v\cdot\partial_{zz}v_{h}\,d\vec{x}+\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}W_{h}\partial_{z}v\cdot\partial_{zz}v_{h}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\alpha\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}Z_{h}(v_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{h}v)\cdot\partial_{zz}v_{h}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\alpha\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}Z_{h}W\partial_{z}v\cdot\partial_{zz}v_{h}\,d\vec{x}=:\sum_{k=30}^{38}I_{k}.

(3.41)

Applying Young’s inequality yields that, for any constant
\delta>0
,

\displaystyle I_{31}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta^{-1}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}.

Now we calculate the terms involving the vertical velocity. After substituting (
3.30
) into
I_{33}
, one has that

\displaystyle I_{33}=-(\alpha+1)\int z^{\alpha}\partial_{z}v_{h}\cdot\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigl(\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z^{\alpha+1}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\int z^{\alpha}\partial_{z}v_{h}\cdot\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigl(v\cdot\nabla_{h}Z^{\alpha+1}+\mathrm{div}_{h}\,vZ^{\alpha+1}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\bigl(\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\bigr)\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\,dz+\bigl(\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{6}}\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+1\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}+\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}.

Now we substitute
\eqref{eq:dh-FBCPE}_{3}
into
I_{36}
, and it follows that,

\displaystyle I_{36}=\int z^{\alpha+1}\partial_{z}v\cdot\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigl((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}Z^{\alpha+1}+\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z^{\alpha+1}

\displaystyle~~~~+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}-\int\partial_{z}v\cdot\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigl((\alpha+1)\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v\,d\xi\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~\times Z^{\alpha+1}+\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v_{h}\,d\xi\cdot\nabla_{h}Z^{\alpha+1}+\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}\,d\xi Z^{\alpha+1}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl((\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2})\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}+\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}+\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}^{2}v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\,dz+\int_{0}^{1}\bigl((\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2})

\displaystyle~~~~\times(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}\bigr)\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\,dz\lesssim\bigl(1+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}.

Next, after substituting (
1.18
) into
I_{38}
, we have that,

\displaystyle I_{38}=\alpha\int z^{\alpha+1}\partial_{z}v\cdot\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigl((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\cdot\nabla_{h}ZZ^{\alpha-1}Z_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}Z^{\alpha}Z_{h}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}-\alpha\int\partial_{z}v\cdot\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigl((\alpha+1)\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v\,d\xi\cdot\nabla_{h}Z

\displaystyle~~~~\times Z^{\alpha-1}Z_{h}+\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\,d\xi Z^{\alpha}Z_{h}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\bigl(\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{8}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}\bigr|_{L^{8}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{8}}\bigr)\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\,dz

\displaystyle~~~~+\int_{0}^{1}\bigl((\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{8}}^{2}+(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{8}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{8}}\bigr)\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\,dz\lesssim\bigl(1+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}.

On the other hand, applying Hölder’s, Hardy’s (Lemma
2
), and the Sobolev embedding inequalities, yields that for some
\delta\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle I_{34}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{t}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{-1}\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{-1}\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta^{-1}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}},

where we have used the inequality,

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert z^{-1}\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}=\int_{0}^{1}z^{-2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{h}-\partial_{z}v_{h}|_{z=0}\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dz

(3.42)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dz=\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}.

To finish the proof of this proposition, we list the estimates for the rest in (
3.3
):

\displaystyle I_{30}\lesssim\int\bigl(\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl|\nabla_{h}v_{z}\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\,dz\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+1)\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{32}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}},

\displaystyle I_{35}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{37}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}+\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}.

Summing up the inequalities above with suitably small
\delta
will yield the following inequality,

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\biggl\{\dfrac{1}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|v_{hz}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}\biggr\}+\int\biggl(Z\bigl(\mu\bigl|\nabla_{h}\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr|^{2}

(3.43)

\displaystyle+(\mu+\lambda)\bigl|\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr|^{2}+\mu/2\bigl|\partial_{zz}v_{h}\bigr|^{2}\bigr)\biggr)\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{3}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\mathcal{H}(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}},\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}},\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}})

\displaystyle\times\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr).

Then integrating this inequality with respect to the temporal variable, together with (
3.40
) completes the proof of (
3.36
).
∎

3.4
Stability

This section is devoted to show Proposition
3
. Throughout the following arguments, we use
0<C<\infty
to denote a positive constant independent of the solution
(Z,v)
, which depends on the initial data and might be different from line to line. Summing up (
4
), (
3.5
), (
3.6
), (
3.14
), (
3.19
), (
3.23
), (
3.24
), (
3.35
), and (
3.36
), yields the inequality

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\mathfrak{E}_{c}(t)+\int_{0}^{T}\mathfrak{D}(t)\,dt\leq C({\Arrowvert Z\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}},\Arrowvert Z^{-1}\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}})\mathcal{E}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{E}(t))

(3.44)

\displaystyle\times\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert Z-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr),\partial_{t},

where
0<C({\Arrowvert Z\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}},\Arrowvert Z^{-1}\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}})<\infty
is a constant depending on
\Arrowvert Z\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}},\Arrowvert Z^{-1}\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}<\infty
. Then, if
\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\mathcal{E}(t)<\varepsilon_{2}<\varepsilon_{1}
for some sufficiently small
0<\varepsilon_{2}<\varepsilon_{1}
with
\varepsilon_{1}
given in Proposition
1
, one has that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{E}(t))\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert Z-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr)\,dt\leq\dfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{T}\mathfrak{D}(t)\,dt,

and

\displaystyle|Z-1|\leq C\varepsilon_{1}^{1/2}<1/2,

where we have applied (
2.5
) and (
2.10
). Therefore (
3.44
) yields,

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\mathfrak{E}_{c}(t)+\dfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{T}\mathfrak{D}(t)\,dt\leq C\mathcal{E}_{0}.

Furthermore, taking
c=\frac{\mu}{8g}
as in Proposition
1
, together with (
2.6
) and (
2.10
), will finish the proof of Proposition
3
.

4
Regularity up to the vacuum boundary

In this section, we study the regularity of
v
near the vacuum boundary
z=0
. We will make use of the viscosity to obtain the
L^{\infty}(0,T;H^{2}(\Omega))
estimates for
v
. In fact, we will show the following proposition:

Proposition 10
.

Under the same assumptions as in Proposition
3
,
there is a constant
\varepsilon_{3}>0
such that if
\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\mathcal{E}(t)\leq\varepsilon_{3}
, then the following inequality holds:

\bigl\Arrowvert v(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\leq C\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)+1\bigr)\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{1/2},

(4.1)

for some constant
0<C<\infty
and any
0\leq t\leq T
.

Proof.

Directly from
\eqref{FB-CPE}_{2}
, one has

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert\mu\Delta_{h}v+(\mu+\lambda)\nabla_{h}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v+\mu\partial_{zz}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

(4.2)

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\mu(\nabla_{h}\log Z)\cdot\nabla_{h}v+(\mu+\lambda)(\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v)\nabla_{h}\log Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}\partial_{t}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}v\cdot\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}W\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}.

On the one hand, applying integration by parts in (
4.2
) yields

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert\mu\Delta_{h}v+(\mu+\lambda)\nabla_{h}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v+\mu\partial_{zz}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}=\mu^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\mu^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\mu+\lambda)^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\mu(\mu+\lambda)\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+2\mu^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\gtrsim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}.

On the other hand, we will show that the right-hand side of (
4.2
) can be hounded by
\mathcal{E}(t)
. In fact, after substituting (
1.18
), one can check that

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}W\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\int\biggl[z^{2\alpha+2}\bigl(\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|^{2}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z^{\alpha}\bigr|^{2}+\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}\bigr|^{2}\bigl|Z\bigr|^{2\alpha}\bigr)\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v\,d\xi\bigr)^{2}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z^{\alpha}\bigr|^{2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}+\bigl(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\,d\xi\bigr)^{2}\bigl|Z\bigr|^{2\alpha}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}\biggr]\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\biggl[\bigl(\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigl|Z^{2\alpha-2}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\bigl|Z\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2\alpha}\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\,d\xi\bigl|Z^{2\alpha-2}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\,d\xi\bigl|Z\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2\alpha}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl|z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\biggr]\,dz\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert Z^{2\alpha-2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}^{2\alpha}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2}\leq C_{\Arrowvert Z\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}},\Arrowvert Z^{-1}\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}}\bigl(\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{3}+\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{2}\bigr).

Similarly, the estimates for the rest terms in (
4.2
) are listed in the following:

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert\mu\nabla_{h}\log Z\cdot\nabla_{h}v+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\nabla_{h}\log Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert Z^{-1}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}\leq C({\Arrowvert Z^{-1}\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}})\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{1/2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}},

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}\partial_{t}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\leq C({\Arrowvert Z\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}})\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{1/2},

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}v\cdot\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\leq C({\Arrowvert Z\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}})\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\leq C({\Arrowvert Z\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}})\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\leq C({\Arrowvert Z\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}})\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{1/2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}.

Therefore, (
4.2
) yields

\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla^{2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\leq C({\Arrowvert Z\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}},\Arrowvert Z^{-1}\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}})\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{1/2}\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+1\bigr).

This concludes the proof of (
4.1
) after choosing
\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\mathcal{E}(t)<\varepsilon_{3}
sufficiently small.
∎

Next, we will derive estimate for
\partial_{zzz}v
.
After applying
\partial_{z}
to
\eqref{FB-CPE}_{2}
, one has the following equation:

\displaystyle\mu\partial_{zzz}v=-\mu\Delta_{h}v_{z}-(\mu+\lambda)\nabla_{h}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{z}-\mu\nabla_{h}\log Z\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{z}

(4.3)

\displaystyle-(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{z}\nabla_{h}\log Z+\alpha z^{\alpha-1}Z^{\alpha}\bigl(\partial_{t}v+v\cdot\nabla_{h}v\bigr)

\displaystyle+z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}\bigl(\partial_{t}v_{z}+v_{z}\cdot\nabla_{h}v+v\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{z}+W\partial_{z}v_{z}\bigr)+\partial_{z}(z^{\alpha}W)Z^{\alpha}\partial_{z}v

\displaystyle+\alpha gz^{\alpha-1}Z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}Z.

For the sake of convenience, in the following arguments, we recall again assumption (
3.2
), i.e., that
1/2\leq\bigl|Z-1\bigr|\leq 2
.
Hence we have

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert\mu\Delta_{h}v_{z}+(\mu+\lambda)\nabla_{h}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}},

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert\mu\nabla_{h}\log Z\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{z}+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{z}\nabla_{h}\log Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{1/2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}},

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}\partial_{t}v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}v_{z}\cdot\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{1/2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}},

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}v\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{1/2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}.

On the other hand, after substituting (
3.30
) into the following, one can easily check that

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}(z^{\alpha}W)Z^{\alpha}\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\lesssim\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)+\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{1/2}\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\bigr).

Moreover, after using (
1.18
), as before, one concludes that

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}W\partial_{z}v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\int\biggl(z^{2\alpha+2}\bigl(\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|^{2}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z^{\alpha}\bigr|^{2}+\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}\bigr|^{2}\bigl|Z\bigr|^{2\alpha}\bigr)\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{z}\bigr|^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v\,d\xi\bigr)^{2}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z^{\alpha}\bigr|^{2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{z}\bigr|^{2}+\bigl(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\,d\xi\bigr)^{2}\bigl|Z\bigr|^{2\alpha}\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{z}\bigr|^{2}\biggr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\biggl(\bigl(\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigl|Z^{2\alpha-2}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\bigl|Z\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2\alpha}\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{z}\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl[\bigl(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\,d\xi\bigr)\bigl|Z^{2\alpha-2}\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\bigl(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\,d\xi\bigr)\bigl|Z\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}^{2\alpha}\bigr]

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{z}\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\biggr)\,dz\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+1\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zz}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl(\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{2}+\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}\bigr).

Next, if
\alpha>1/2
, (or equivalently
2\alpha-2>-1
), i.e.,
1<\gamma<3
, we have,

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha-1}Z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}=\int_{0}^{1}z^{2\alpha-2}\,dz\times\bigl|Z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha-1}Z^{\alpha}\partial_{t}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}z^{2\alpha-2}\bigl|\partial_{t}v\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dz\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{t}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla\partial_{t}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha-1}Z^{\alpha}v\cdot\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}z^{2\alpha-2}\bigl|v\cdot\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dz\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}z^{2\alpha}\bigl(\bigl|v\cdot\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl|\partial_{z}(v\cdot\nabla_{h}v)\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr)\,dz\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert v\cdot\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\cdot\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert v\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}^{2}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{E}(t)\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2},

where we have applied Hölder’s, Hardy’s (Lemma
2
), and the Sobolev embedding inequalities.
Therefore, after summing up the above inequalities, one has, for
\alpha>1/2
,

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zzz}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{t}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)+\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{1/2}\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\bigr).

On the other hand, if
0<\alpha\leq 1/2
, i.e.,
\gamma\geq 3
, let
\beta
be any number such that
2\alpha+2\beta-2>-1
. Then the same arguments as above show that

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\beta}\partial_{zzz}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{t}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)+\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{1/2}\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\bigr).

Therefore, we have demonstrated the following proposition:

Proposition 11
.

Under the same assumptions as in Proposition
3
, one has

\displaystyle\bullet\text{if $\alpha>1/2$, i.e., $1<\gamma<3$},~\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{zzz}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dt\leq\int_{0}^{T}\biggl[\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}

(4.4)

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{t}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl(\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{2}+\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}\bigr)\biggr]\,dt;

\displaystyle\bullet\text{if $0<\alpha\leq 1/2$, i.e., $\gamma\geq 3$},~\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\beta}\partial_{zzz}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dt\leq\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{t}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl(\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{2}+\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)

\displaystyle\times\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}\bigr)\,dt,

for any
\beta>\frac{1}{2}-\alpha
. In particular,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{1/2}\partial_{zzz}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dt\leq\int_{0}^{T}\biggl[\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{t}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

(4.5)

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl(\bigl(\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)^{2}+\mathcal{E}(t)\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}\bigr)\biggr]\,dt.

5
Global well-posedness and stability of the stationary equilibrium state

In this section, we will establish the global well-posedness and the stability of the stationary equilibrium state of the compressible primitive equation (
1.16
).

5.1
Local well-posedness theory

We first show the local-in-time well-posedness theory of (
1.16
) with the given initial data
(Z_{0},v_{0})\in H^{2}(\Omega)\times H^{2}(\Omega)
. For this purpose, we introduce the following “linearized” system associated with (
1.16
), given
(\hat{Z},\hat{v})
smooth enough, to be specified later:

\begin{cases}\partial_{t}Z+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}\hat{v}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}}\hat{Z}=0,\\
(z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}^{\alpha}+\eta)\partial_{t}v+z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}^{\alpha}\bigl(\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{v}+\hat{W}\partial_{z}\hat{v}+g\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr)=\mu\Delta_{h}v\\
~~~~+(\mu+\lambda)\nabla_{h}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v+\mu\partial_{zz}v+\mu\nabla_{h}\log\hat{Z}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\\
~~~~+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}\nabla_{h}\log\hat{Z},\\
\partial_{z}Z=0,\end{cases}

(5.1)

for any fixed positive constant
\eta\in(0,1)
, where
\hat{W}
is given by

\displaystyle z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}\hat{W}=z^{\alpha+1}\bigl((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}\hat{v}}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}}\hat{Z}\bigr)

(5.2)

\displaystyle-(\alpha+1)\bigl(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\hat{v}\,d\xi\bigr)\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}-\bigl(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}\,d\xi\bigr)\hat{Z}.

Also, the following boundary condition is imposed

\partial_{z}v|_{z=0,1}=0.

In the following, we will study (
5.1
) with the given approximating initial data
(Z,v)\big|_{t=0}=(Z_{0,\eta},v_{0,\eta})
. The approximating initial data satisfies

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{0,\eta}-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}},\bigl\Arrowvert v_{0,\eta}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}<\infty,

\displaystyle(Z_{0,\eta},v_{0,\eta})\rightarrow(Z_{0},v_{0}),~~\text{as $\eta\rightarrow 0^{+}$ in $H^{2}(\Omega)\times H^{2}(\Omega)$}.

Also we denote the approximating total initial energy as

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta}:=\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{0,\eta}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{1,\eta}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

(5.3)

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{0,\eta,z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert v_{0,\eta}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{0,\eta}-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{1,\eta}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{0}<\infty,

where
v_{1,\eta}=v_{t}|_{t=0},Z_{1,\eta}=Z_{t}|_{t=0}
are given by

\displaystyle Z_{1,\eta}+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{0,\eta}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{0,\eta}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{0,\eta}}Z_{0,\eta}=0,

\displaystyle(z^{\alpha}Z_{0,\eta}^{\alpha}+\eta)v_{1,\eta}+z^{\alpha}Z_{0,\eta}^{\alpha}\bigl(v_{0,\eta}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{0,\eta}+W_{0,\eta}\partial_{z}v_{0,\eta}+g\nabla_{h}Z_{0,\eta}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~=\mu\Delta_{h}v_{0,\eta}+(\mu+\lambda)\nabla_{h}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{0,\eta}+\mu\partial_{zz}v_{0,\eta}+\mu\nabla_{h}\log Z_{0,\eta}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{0,\eta}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{0,\eta}\nabla_{h}\log Z_{0,\eta},

\displaystyle z^{\alpha}Z_{0,\eta}W_{0,\eta}=z^{\alpha+1}\bigl((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{0,\eta}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{0,\eta}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{0,\eta}}Z_{0,\eta}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~-(\alpha+1)\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v_{0,\eta}\,d\xi\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{0,\eta}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{0,\eta}\,d\xi Z_{0,\eta}.

Recall that
\mathcal{E}_{0}
is given in (
2.3
).
Then as
\eta\rightarrow 0^{+}
, it is easy to verify
\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta}\rightarrow\mathcal{E}_{0}
. Accordingly, the approximating total energy functional is defined as,

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\eta}(t):=\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2}

(5.4)

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}.

We will establish the local well-posedness of (
1.16
) by a fixed point argument. In fact, the well-posedness of the “linearized” system (
5.1
) follows from the standard existence theory of hyperbolic-parabolic systems with
(\hat{Z},\hat{v})
given and smooth enough (cf.
[
11
]
). In the following, we show some estimates for the solution
(Z,v)
to (
5.1
) in a small time interval which are independent of
\eta
; and show that the map
\Phi:(\hat{Z},\hat{v})\mapsto(Z,v)
has a fixed point
(Z_{\eta},v_{\eta})
in some functional space via the Tychonoff fixed point theorem. Then taking the limit
\eta\rightarrow 0^{+}
yields the existence of local solution to (
1.16
), since our estimates are independent of
\eta
. Such a procedure is similar to that presented in our previous work
[
39
]
. In the following, we will only sketch the estimates and show that they are independent of
\eta
. In fact, we will work in the following functional space:

\displaystyle X_{T}:=\bigl\{(Z,v)\in H^{2}(\Omega)\times H^{2}(\Omega)|(Z,v)|_{t=0}=(Z_{0,\eta},v_{0,\eta}),~\bigl|Z-1\bigr|\leq\frac{1}{2},

(5.5)

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\mathcal{E}_{\eta}(t)+\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dt

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta}\bigr\},

for some
T,C_{0}\in(0,\infty)
to be specified later. Note that,
X_{T}
is convex and compact in the
L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega))\times L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega))
topology. We will also require
\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta}
to be small. Let
(\hat{Z},\hat{v})\in X_{T}
and
(Z,v)
be the corresponding solution to (
5.1
) which is guaranteed to exist by
[
11
]
. For the sake of convenience, we denote the total energy functional for
(\hat{Z},\hat{v})
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{\hat{E}}_{\eta}:=\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}\hat{v}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}\hat{v}_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Also, we will frequently use (
3.33
), (
3.42
), and the following inequality, which resembles (
2.2
) and (
3.16
),

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert f\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}f\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla f\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(5.6)

Estimates for
Z

We start with the estimates for
Z
by presenting the
H^{2}
estimates for
Z-1
. First, multiplying
\eqref{eq:linearized-FBCPE}_{1}
with
2(Z-1)
and integrating the resultant yield

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\bigl\Arrowvert Z-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}=(\alpha+1)\int\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}}\bigl|Z-1\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}-2\int\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}}\hat{Z}(Z-1)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert Z-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\bigr)\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert Z-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}.

Then applying Grönwall’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\sup_{0<t<T}\bigl\Arrowvert Z-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq e^{C\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\,dt}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{0,\eta}-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

(5.7)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\,dt\bigr)\leq e^{CC_{0}^{1/2}\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta}^{1/2}T^{1/2}}\bigl(\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta}+C_{0}^{1/2}\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta}^{1/2}T^{1/2}\bigr)

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta},

provided
T
small enough. On the other hand, after applying
\partial_{hh}
to
\eqref{eq:linearized-FBCPE}_{1}
, it holds

\displaystyle\partial_{t}Z_{hh}+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}\hat{v}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{hh}+2(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}\hat{v}_{h}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}\hat{v}_{hh}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}}\hat{Z}_{hh}+2\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}_{h}}\hat{Z}_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}_{hh}}\hat{Z}=0.

Taking the
L^{2}
-inner product of the above equation with
2Z_{hh}
yields

\dfrac{d}{dt}\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}.

To close the above estimate, we observe that similar arguments also hold for
Z_{h}
. That is, we have

\dfrac{d}{dt}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}.

Therefore, Gönwall’s inequality implies that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\leq e^{C\int_{0}^{T}(1+\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}})\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\,dt}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z_{0,\eta}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}

(5.8)

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{T}(1+\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}})\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\,dt\bigr)\leq 2\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta},

for
T
small enough. Furthermore, from
\eqref{eq:linearized-FBCPE}_{1}
, similar arguments as those established in (
3.13
) yield that

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

(5.9)

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta}+\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta}^{1/2}.

Estimates for
v

The
L^{2}
estimate for
v

We start with the
L^{2}
estimate for
v
. After taking inner product of
2v
with
\eqref{eq:linearized-FBCPE}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+2(\mu+\lambda)\bigl\Arrowvert\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

(5.10)

\displaystyle+2\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}=\int\alpha z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha-1}\hat{Z}_{t}\bigl|v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}-\int 2z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\cdot v

\displaystyle+2gz^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\cdot v\,d\vec{x}-2\int z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\hat{W}\partial_{z}\hat{v}\cdot v\,d\vec{x}+2\int\bigl(\mu\nabla_{h}\log\hat{Z}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{v}

\displaystyle+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}\nabla_{h}\log\hat{Z}\bigr)\cdot v\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\bigl(1+\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}-2\int z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\hat{W}\partial_{z}\hat{v}\cdot v\,d\vec{x}.

Meanwhile, after plugging (
5.2
) into the following term and applying Hölder’s, Minkowski’s, and the Sobolev embedding inequalities, one has that

\displaystyle-2\int z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\hat{W}\partial_{z}\hat{v}\cdot v\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\biggl[z^{\alpha/2}\bigl|\partial_{z}\hat{v}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\bigl|v\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl(\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\hat{v}}\bigr|_{L^{8}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr|_{L^{8}}+\bigl|\overline{z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}\hat{v}}\bigr|_{L^{4}}

(5.11)

\displaystyle+(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|\hat{v}\bigr|_{L^{8}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}\bigl|\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr|_{L^{8}}+(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\,d\xi)^{1/2}\bigr)\biggr]\,dz

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}.

Therefore, after substituting (
5.11
) into (
5.10
), one integrates the resulting equation of (

) with respect to the temporal variable to obtain:

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}\biggl[2\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+2(\mu+\lambda)\bigl\Arrowvert\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

(5.12)

\displaystyle+2\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\biggr]\,dt\leq\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}Z_{0,\eta}^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{0,\eta}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{\hat{E}}_{\eta})\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+1\bigr)\,dt+CT.

On the other hand, taking the
L^{2}
-inner product of
\eqref{eq:linearized-FBCPE}_{2}
with
