yields,

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\bigl(\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bigl\Arrowvert\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\lambda\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr)

(5.13)

\displaystyle+2\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}=-2\int z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\cdot v_{t}+gz^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\cdot v_{t}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle-2\int z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\hat{W}\partial_{z}\hat{v}\cdot v_{t}\,d\vec{x}+2\int\bigl(\mu\nabla_{h}\log\hat{Z}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{v}

\displaystyle+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}\nabla_{h}\log\hat{Z}\bigr)\cdot v_{t}\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigr)

\displaystyle-2\int z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\hat{W}\partial_{z}\hat{v}\cdot v_{t}\,d\vec{x}.

Similarly as before, applying Hölder’s and Minkowski’s inequalities implies that

\displaystyle-2\int z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\hat{W}\partial_{z}\hat{v}\cdot v_{t}\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}

(5.14)

\displaystyle\times\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}.

Then substituting (

) into (

) and integrating the resulting inequality yields that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\bigl\{\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bigl\Arrowvert\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr\}

(5.15)

\displaystyle+2\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dt\leq\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{0,\eta}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+(\mu+\lambda)\bigl\Arrowvert\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{0,\eta}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\lambda\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v_{0,\eta}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\omega\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dt+\int_{0}^{T}\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{\hat{E}}_{\eta},1/\omega)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\bigr)\,dt,

for any
\omega\in(0,1)
.

The temporal derivative of
v

After applying
\partial_{t}
to
\eqref{eq:linearized-FBCPE}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle(z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}^{\alpha}+\eta)\partial_{t}v_{t}=\mu\Delta_{h}v_{t}+(\mu+\lambda)\nabla_{h}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{t}+\mu\partial_{zz}v_{t}

\displaystyle~~~~-\alpha z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}^{\alpha-1}\hat{Z}_{t}\partial_{t}v-z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}^{\alpha}\bigl(\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{v}_{t}+\hat{v}_{t}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{v}+g\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}_{t}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~-z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}^{\alpha}\bigl(\hat{W}\partial_{z}\hat{v}_{t}+\hat{W}_{t}\partial_{z}\hat{v}\bigr)-\alpha z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}^{\alpha-1}\hat{Z}_{t}\bigl(\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{v}+g\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~-\alpha z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}^{\alpha-1}\hat{Z}_{t}\hat{W}\partial_{z}\hat{v}+\mu\nabla_{h}(\log\hat{Z})_{t}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{v}+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}\nabla_{h}(\log\hat{Z})_{t}

\displaystyle~~~~+\mu\nabla_{h}\log\hat{Z}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{v}_{t}+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}_{t}\nabla_{h}\log\hat{Z}.

Taking the

-inner product of the above equation with

and applying integration by parts in the resultant yield that

\displaystyle~~~~+2\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}=-\alpha\int z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}^{\alpha-1}\hat{Z}_{t}\bigl|\partial_{t}v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}-2\int\bigl(z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}^{\alpha}\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{v}_{t}\cdot v_{t}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-2\int\bigl(z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}^{\alpha}\hat{v}_{t}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\cdot v_{t}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}+2g\int z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}^{\alpha-1}\hat{Z}_{t}(\alpha\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\cdot v_{t}+{\hat{Z}}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{t})\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-2\int z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\hat{W}\partial_{z}\hat{v}_{t}\cdot v_{t}\,d\vec{x}-2\int z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}^{\alpha}\hat{W}_{t}\partial_{z}\hat{v}\cdot v_{t}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-2\alpha\int\bigl(z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha-1}\hat{Z}_{t}\hat{W}\partial_{z}\hat{v}\cdot v_{t}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}-2\alpha\int\bigl[z^{\alpha}{\hat{Z}}^{\alpha-1}\hat{Z}_{t}(\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{v}+g\nabla_{h}\hat{Z})\cdot v_{t}\bigr]\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-2\int\bigl[\mu(\log\hat{Z})_{t}\Delta_{h}\hat{v}+(\mu+\lambda)(\log\hat{Z})_{t}\nabla_{h}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}\bigr]\cdot v_{t}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-2\int\bigl[\mu(\log\hat{Z})_{t}\nabla_{h}\hat{v}:\nabla_{h}v_{t}+(\mu+\lambda)(\log\hat{Z})_{t}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{t}\bigr]\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~=:\sum_{k=1}^{11}L_{k}.

Similarly as before, the estimates for
L_{k}
’s are as follows: for every
\delta,\omega\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle L_{1}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+(C_{\delta}\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{4}+\delta)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle L_{2}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\omega\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+(C_{\delta,\omega}\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{4}+\delta)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle L_{3}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\omega\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+(C_{\delta,\omega}\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{4}+\omega)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\hat{v}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle L_{4}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle L_{5}=-2\int\bigl(\hat{Z}^{\alpha-1}\partial_{z}\hat{v}_{t}\cdot v_{t}\bigr)\bigl[z^{\alpha+1}\bigl((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}\hat{v}}\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}}\hat{Z}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~-(\alpha+1)(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\hat{v}\,d\xi)\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}-(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}\,d\xi)\hat{Z}\bigr]\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl(\delta+C_{\delta}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+1\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla\hat{v}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle L_{6}=2(\alpha+1)\int\biggl[(\partial_{z}\hat{v}\cdot v_{t})\hat{Z}^{\alpha}(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}\,d\xi)(\log\hat{Z})_{t}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+\alpha(\partial_{z}\hat{v}\cdot v_{t})\hat{Z}^{\alpha-1}(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}\hat{v}}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\hat{v}\,d\xi)\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}(\log\hat{Z})_{t}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+\hat{Z}^{\alpha}(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}\hat{v}}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\hat{v}\,d\xi)\cdot\nabla_{h}(\partial_{z}\hat{v}\cdot v_{t})(\log\hat{Z})_{t}\biggr]\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-2(\alpha+1)\int\biggl[(\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}\hat{v}\cdot v_{t})(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}\hat{v}_{t}}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\hat{v}_{t}\,d\xi)\cdot\nabla_{h}\log\hat{Z}\biggr]\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-2\int\biggl[(\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}\hat{v}\cdot v_{t})(z^{\alpha+1}\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}_{t}}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}_{t}\,d\xi)\biggr]\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~-(\alpha+1)(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\hat{v}\,d\xi)\cdot\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}-(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}\,d\xi)\hat{Z}\bigr)\biggr]\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{v}_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2},

\displaystyle L_{8}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+C_{\delta}\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2},

\displaystyle L_{9}+L_{10}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl\Arrowvert\hat{Z}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle L_{11}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla\hat{v}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

where we have applied integration by parts, Hölder’s, Minkowski’s, the Sobolev embedding inequalities, and
the fact that from (
5.2

\displaystyle z^{\alpha}\hat{W}_{t}=z^{\alpha+1}\bigl((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}\hat{v}}\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log\hat{Z})_{t}+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}\hat{v}_{t}}\cdot\nabla_{h}\log\hat{Z}

\displaystyle~~~~+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}_{t}}\bigr)-(\alpha+1)(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\hat{v}\,d\xi)\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log\hat{Z})_{t}

\displaystyle~~~~-(\alpha+1)(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\hat{v}_{t}\,d\xi)\cdot\nabla_{h}\log\hat{Z}-(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,\hat{v}_{t}\,d\xi).

Then after summing up the above inequalities with small enough
\delta>0
and every
\omega\in(0,1)
and integrating the resultant in the temporal variable, it follows that,

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{t}(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dt

(5.16)

\displaystyle\leq\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}Z_{0}^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{1,\eta}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{\hat{E}}_{\eta},\omega)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla\hat{v}_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{v}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}\bigr)\,dt+\int_{0}^{T}\biggl(\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{\hat{E}}_{\eta},1/\omega,1)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{\hat{E}}_{\eta},1/\omega,\omega)\biggr)\,dt.

The horizontal spatial derivatives of
v

After applying
\partial_{hh}
to
\eqref{eq:linearized-FBCPE}_{2}

\displaystyle=-2\alpha z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha-1}\hat{Z}_{h}\partial_{t}v_{h}-\alpha z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha-1}\hat{Z}_{hh}\partial_{t}v-\alpha(\alpha-1)z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha-2}(\hat{Z}_{h})^{2}\partial_{t}v

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+g\nabla_{h}\hat{Z})\bigr)\cdot v_{hhh}\biggr]\,d\vec{x}+2\int\biggl[\partial_{h}(z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\hat{W}\partial_{z}\hat{v})\cdot v_{hhh}\biggr]\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~=:\sum_{k=12}^{17}L_{k}.

Then applying Hölder’s, Minkowski’s, and the Sobolev embedding inequalities yields that, for every
\delta,\omega\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle L_{12}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl(C_{\delta}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{4}+\delta\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle L_{13}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\omega\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl(C_{\delta,\omega}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{4}+\delta\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle L_{14}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\omega\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl(C_{\delta,\omega}(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{8}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}\hat{Z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{4})+\omega\bigr)

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{hz}(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}\biggl(\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{hz}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v_{hz}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\biggr)\,dt

The fixed point argument

Since
(\hat{Z},\hat{v})\in X_{T}
, (

) and (

) implies that for
\omega
small enough, there are constants

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\bigl\{\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}+\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{t}(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl(C_{0}\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta}+\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dt\bigr)+T\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta},\omega,1/\omega,1)\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\bigl\{\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2}+1\bigr\}+CT.

Then for
\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta}
small enough, taking
\omega
small and then

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\bigl\{\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}+\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{t}(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+C^{\prime}\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{z}(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2}\bigr\}+C^{\prime\prime}\int_{0}^{T}\biggl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert(z^{\alpha}+\eta)^{1/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\biggr)\,dt\leq 4C^{\prime\prime\prime}\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta}.

Therefore, together with (
5.7
), (
5.8
) and (
5.9
), this implies
(Z,v)\in X_{T}
for small enough
\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta},T
and large enough
C_{0}
. This verifies that
\Phi:(\hat{Z},\hat{v})\mapsto(Z,v)
is from
X_{T}
to itself. It is easy to verify that
\Phi
is continuous in the topology of
L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega))\times L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega))
and that
X_{T}
is convex and compact in the
L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega))\times L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega))
topology. Therefore, the Tychonoff fixed point theorem implies that there is a fixed point of
\Phi
in
X_{T}
for small enough
\mathcal{E}_{0,\eta},T
and large enough
C_{0}
. This is the local solution to the following equations,

\begin{cases}\partial_{t}Z+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}Z=0,\\
(z^{\alpha}{Z}^{\alpha}+\eta)\partial_{t}v+z^{\alpha}{Z}^{\alpha}\bigl(v\cdot\nabla_{h}v+W\partial_{z}v+g\nabla_{h}Z\bigr)=\mu\Delta_{h}v\\
~~~~+(\mu+\lambda)\nabla_{h}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v+\mu\partial_{zz}v+\mu\nabla_{h}\log Z\cdot\nabla_{h}v\\
~~~~+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\nabla_{h}\log Z,\\
\partial_{z}Z=0,\\
z^{\alpha}{Z}W=z^{\alpha+1}\bigl((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}Z\bigr)\\
~~~~-(\alpha+1)(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v\,d\xi)\cdot\nabla_{h}Z-(\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\,d\xi)Z.\end{cases}

Notice, the above estimates are independent of
\eta
. Thus by taking
\eta\rightarrow 0^{+}
in the above equations, we achieve the local solutions to (
1.16
) for small initial energy
\mathcal{E}_{0}
given in (
2.3
). In other words, we have shown the following proposition:

Proposition 12
.

There is a constant
\varepsilon_{4}<\varepsilon_{3}
, where
\varepsilon_{3}
is given in Proposition
10
, such that if
\mathcal{E}_{0}<\varepsilon_{4}
small enough, that there are a
T_{\varepsilon_{4}}\in(0,\infty)
and a local strong solution
(Z,v)\in L^{\infty}(0,T_{\varepsilon_{4}};H^{2}(\Omega))\times L^{\infty}(0,T_{\varepsilon_{4}};H^{2}(\Omega))
to (
1.16
). Moreover, there is a constant
0<C_{2}<\infty
such that

\mathcal{E}(t)\leq C_{2}\mathcal{E}_{0},

(5.23)

for
t\in(0,T_{\varepsilon_{4}})
.

5.2
Uniqueness and global stability theory

We summarize the result from Propositions
3
,
10
,
12
in the following proposition.

Proposition 13
.

Consider initial data
(Z_{0},v_{0})\in H^{2}(\Omega)\times H^{2}(\Omega)
satisfying
\mathcal{E}_{0}<\varepsilon^{\prime}
with some
\varepsilon^{\prime}\leq\min\{\varepsilon_{2}/C_{1},\varepsilon_{2}/C_{2},\varepsilon_{4}/C_{1},\varepsilon_{4}/C_{2},\varepsilon_{2},\varepsilon_{4}\}
where
\varepsilon_{2},C_{1}
are given in Proposition
3
and
\varepsilon_{4},C_{2}
is given in Proposition
12
. There exists a unique global strong solution
(Z,v)
to (
1.16
) satisfying estimates (
3.1
) and (
4.1
).

Proof.

The existence of the global strong solution follows by a continuity argument, using the fact that the relevant norms of the solutions remain bounded on any finite maximal interval of existence. What is left is to show the uniqueness.
Consider
(Z_{1},v_{1})
,
(Z_{2},v_{2})
(in this proof, not to get confused with the initial data) being two strong solutions to (
1.16
) with the same initial data. Then, for
i=1,2
, the following equations hold,

\begin{cases}z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}_{i}(\partial_{t}v_{i}+v_{i}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{i}+W_{i}\partial_{z}v_{i}+g\nabla_{h}Z_{i})=\mu\Delta_{h}v_{i}+(\mu+\lambda)\nabla_{h}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{i}\\
~~~~~~~~+\mu\partial_{zz}v_{i}+\mu\nabla_{h}\log Z_{i}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{i}+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{i}\nabla_{h}\log Z_{i},\\
\partial_{t}Z_{i}+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{i}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{i}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{i}}Z_{i}=0,\\
z^{\alpha}W_{i}=z^{\alpha+1}\bigl((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{i}}\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z_{i}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{i}}\bigr)\\
~~~~~~~~-(\alpha+1)\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v_{i}\,d\xi\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z_{i}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{i}\,d\xi,\\
\partial_{z}Z_{i}=0.\end{cases}

Next, we denote the total energy functionals for these two solutions as
\mathcal{E}_{i}(t)
for
i=1,2
, which are given as in (
2.2
),

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{i}(t):=\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{i}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{i,t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{i,z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert v_{i}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{i}-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{i,t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}.

In the meantime, we denote
Z_{12}=Z_{1}-Z_{2},v_{12}=v_{1}-v_{2}
. Thus
(Z_{12},v_{12})|_{t=0}=0
. Then we have the following equations for
(Z_{12},v_{12})
,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}Z_{12}+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{1}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{12}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{1}}Z_{12}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{12}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{2}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{12}}Z_{2},

(5.24)

\displaystyle z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}_{1}\partial_{t}v_{12}-\mu\Delta_{h}v_{12}-(\mu+\lambda)\nabla_{h}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{12}-\mu\partial_{zz}v_{12}

\displaystyle~~~~=-z^{\alpha}(Z^{\alpha}_{1}-Z^{\alpha}_{2})(\partial_{t}v_{2}+v_{2}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{2}+W_{2}\partial_{z}v_{2}+g\nabla_{h}Z_{2})

\displaystyle~~~~-z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}_{1}(v_{12}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{1}+v_{2}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{12}+(W_{1}-W_{2})\partial_{z}v_{1}+W_{2}\partial_{z}v_{12}

\displaystyle~~~~+g\nabla_{h}Z_{12})+\mu\nabla_{h}(\log Z_{1}-\log Z_{2})\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{1}+\mu\nabla_{h}\log Z_{2}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{12}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{1}\nabla_{h}(\log Z_{1}-\log Z_{2})+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{12}\nabla_{h}\log Z_{2},

(5.25)

\displaystyle z^{\alpha}(W_{1}-W_{2})=z^{\alpha+1}\bigl((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{1}}\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log Z_{1}-\log Z_{2})

\displaystyle~~~~+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{12}}\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z_{2}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{12}}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~-(\alpha+1)\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v_{1}\,d\xi\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log Z_{1}-\log Z_{2})

\displaystyle~~~~-(\alpha+1)\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v_{12}\,d\xi\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z_{2}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{12}\,d\xi.

Now we deduce
L^{2}
estimates for (
5.24
) and (
5.25
). It follows that,

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}=(\alpha-1)\int_{\Omega_{h}}\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{1}}\bigl|Z_{12}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}_{h}-\int_{\Omega_{h}}2(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{12}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{2}Z_{12}

\displaystyle~~~~+2\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{12}}Z_{2}Z_{12}\,d\vec{x}_{h}=:G_{1}+G_{2},

and

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}Z^{\alpha/2}_{1}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+2(\mu+\lambda)\bigl\Arrowvert\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+2\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}=-\alpha\int z^{\alpha}Z_{1}^{\alpha-1}Z_{1,t}\bigl|v_{12}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}-2\int z^{\alpha}(Z^{\alpha}_{1}-Z^{\alpha}_{2})(\partial_{t}v_{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+v_{2}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{2}+g\nabla_{h}Z_{2})\cdot v_{12}\,d\vec{x}-2\int z^{\alpha}(Z_{1}^{\alpha}-Z_{2}^{\alpha})W_{2}\partial_{z}v_{2}\cdot v_{12}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-2\int z^{\alpha}Z_{1}^{\alpha}(v_{12}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{1}+v_{2}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{12}+g\nabla_{h}Z_{12})\cdot v_{12}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-2\int z^{\alpha}Z_{1}^{\alpha}(W_{1}-W_{2})\partial_{z}v_{1}\cdot v_{12}\,d\vec{x}+\int Z_{1}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}(z^{\alpha}W_{2})\bigl|v_{12}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+2\int\mu\nabla_{h}(\log Z_{1}-\log Z_{2})\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{1}\cdot v_{12}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{1}\nabla_{h}(\log Z_{1}-\log Z_{2})\cdot v_{12}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+2\int\mu\nabla_{h}\log Z_{2}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{12}\cdot v_{12}+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{12}\nabla_{h}\log Z_{2}\cdot v_{12}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~=:\sum_{k=3}^{10}G_{k}.

Notice that
(Z_{i},v_{i})
are the solutions that were established before, satisfying

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\mathcal{E}_{i}(t)+\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{i}-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{i,t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{i}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{i}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{i,t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{i,t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dt<C\mathcal{E}_{0},

\displaystyle\bigl|Z_{i}-1\bigr|\leq 1/2.

Then we have that, for any
\delta\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle G_{1}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{1}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle G_{2}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+1\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr),

\displaystyle G_{3}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}+\bigl(\delta+C_{\delta}\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{1,t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{4}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle G_{4}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\partial_{t}v_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla\partial_{t}v_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert v_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle G_{5}=-2\int\biggl((Z_{1}^{\alpha}-Z_{2}^{\alpha})\partial_{z}v_{2}\cdot v_{12}\bigl[z^{\alpha+1}((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{2}}\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z_{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{2}})-(\alpha+1)\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v_{2}\,d\xi\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z_{2}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{2}\,d\xi\bigr]\biggr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle G_{6}=-2\int(z^{\alpha}Z_{1}^{\alpha}v_{12}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{1})\cdot v_{12}\,d\vec{x}+\int z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,(Z_{1}^{\alpha}v_{2})\bigl|v_{12}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+2g\int z^{\alpha}\bigl(Z_{12}\nabla_{h}Z_{1}^{\alpha}\cdot v_{12}+Z_{1}^{\alpha}Z_{12}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{12}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z_{1}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert v_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{1}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z_{1}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle G_{7}=-2\int\biggl(Z_{1}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}v_{1}\cdot v_{12}\bigl[z^{\alpha+1}((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{1}}\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log Z_{1}-\log Z_{2})

\displaystyle~~~~+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{12}}\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z_{2}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{12}})

\displaystyle~~~~-(\alpha+1)\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v_{1}\,d\xi\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log Z_{1}-\log Z_{2})

\displaystyle~~~~-(\alpha+1)\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v_{12}\,d\xi\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z_{2}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{12}\,d\xi\bigr]\biggr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~=-2\int\biggl(Z_{1}^{\alpha}\partial_{z}v_{1}\cdot v_{12}\bigl[z^{\alpha+1}((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{12}}\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z_{2}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{12}})

\displaystyle~~~~-(\alpha+1)\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v_{12}\,d\xi\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z_{2}-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{12}\,d\xi\bigr]\biggr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+2(\alpha+1)\int\biggl(z^{\alpha+1}(\log Z_{1}-\log Z_{2})\mathrm{div}_{h}\,(Z_{1}^{\alpha}(\partial_{z}v_{1}\cdot v_{12})\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{1}})\biggr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-2(\alpha+1)\int\biggl((\log Z_{1}-\log Z_{2})\mathrm{div}_{h}\,[Z_{1}^{\alpha}(\partial_{z}v_{1}\cdot v_{12})\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v_{1}\,d\xi]\biggr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{1}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z_{1}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{1}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{1}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle G_{8}=\int\biggl(Z_{1}^{\alpha}\bigl|v_{12}\bigr|^{2}\bigl[(\alpha+1)z^{\alpha}((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{2}}\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z_{2}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{2}})

\displaystyle~~~~-(\alpha+1)z^{\alpha}v_{2}\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z_{2}-z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{2}\bigr]\biggr)\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert v_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle G_{9}=-2\mu\int\biggl((\log Z_{1}-\log Z_{2})(\Delta_{h}v_{1}\cdot v_{12}+\nabla_{h}v_{1}\otimes\nabla_{h}v_{12})\biggr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-2(\mu+\lambda)\int\biggl((\log Z_{1}-\log Z_{2})(v_{12}\cdot\nabla_{h}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{1}

\displaystyle~~~~+\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{1}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{12})\biggr)\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+C_{\delta}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{1}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Moreover, for
\omega>0
, small enough,

\displaystyle G_{10}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\bigr)\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl((\omega^{-1-\max\{[\alpha/2]-1,0\}/2}+\omega^{-\alpha/4})\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}+\omega^{1/2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{1/2}\bigr)\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+C_{\delta}\mathcal{H}(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}})\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

where we have applied the following inequality with small enough
\omega=\omega(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{-1},\delta)
in
G_{10}
,

\displaystyle\bigl\Arrowvert v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}=\int_{0}^{\omega}\bigl|v_{12}\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dz+\int_{\omega}^{1}\bigl|v_{12}\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dz\lesssim\bigl(\omega^{-4-2\max\{[\alpha/2]-1,0\}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\omega^{-\alpha}\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\omega^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

in which we use Hardy’s inequality as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\omega}\bigl|v_{12}\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dz\lesssim\omega^{-2}\int_{0}^{\omega}z^{2}\bigl|v_{12}\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dz+\omega^{2}\int_{0}^{\omega}\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{12}\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dz

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\omega^{-4}\int_{0}^{\omega}z^{4}\bigl|v_{12}\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dz+\omega^{2}\int_{0}^{\omega}\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{12}\bigr|_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dz

\displaystyle\underbrace{\lesssim\cdots\lesssim}_{\max\{[\alpha/2]-1,0\}~\text{times}}\omega^{-4-2\max\{[\alpha/2]-1,0\}}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\omega^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Therefore, summing up the above inequalities and equations with small enough
\delta>0
yields that for some constant
C_{\mu,\lambda}>0
,

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\bigl\{\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\mu,\lambda}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}Z_{1}^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr\}+\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\mu\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{z}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{E}_{1}(t),\mathcal{E}_{2}(t),1)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{1}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{1}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla\partial_{t}v_{2}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}Z_{1}^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr).

Then after applying Grönwall’s inequality to the above inequality, one has

\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\equiv 0\quad\text{and}\quad\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}Z_{1}^{\alpha/2}v_{12}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\equiv 0.

Therefore
(Z_{1},v_{1})\equiv(Z_{2},v_{2})
. This finishes the proof of the proposition.
∎

6
Decay estimates and asymptotic stability

In this section, we will establish the asymptotic stability theory. In fact, we will show the following proposition:

Proposition 14
.

Under the same assumptions as in Proposition
3
and
0<\alpha<3
, there is a constant
0<\varepsilon_{5}<\varepsilon_{2}
where
\varepsilon_{2}
is given in Proposition
3
, such that if
\mathcal{E}(t)<\varepsilon_{5}
, for
t\in(0,T)
, the following inequality holds,

\mathcal{E}(t)\leq e^{-C_{1}t}C_{2}\mathcal{E}_{0},

(6.1)

for some positive constants
0<C_{1},C_{2}<\infty
.

In order to show Proposition
14
, we need the following lemma concerning the Poincaré type inequality.

Lemma 3
.

Under the same assumption as in Lemma
4
and
0<\alpha<3
,
we have

\displaystyle\int z^{\alpha}\bigl|v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}\leq C\int z^{\beta}\bigl(\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}+\bigl|\partial_{zz}v\bigr|^{2}+\bigl|\partial_{zzz}v\bigr|^{2}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}

(6.2)

\displaystyle+C\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert Z^{\alpha+1}-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2},

for some positive constant
0<C<\infty
and
\beta
satisfying,

0\leq\beta<4-\alpha.

In particular, one can choose
\beta=1
.

Proof.

First, as we have mentioned in the introduction, from
\eqref{FB-CPE}_{2}
, (
3.8
), the conservation of momentum holds,

\dfrac{d}{dt}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}v\,d\vec{x}=0.

Therefore, we have from (
1.21
),

\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}v\,d\vec{x}=\int z^{\alpha}Z_{0}^{\alpha+1}v_{0}\,d\vec{x}=0,

or

\int z^{\alpha}v\,d\vec{x}=\int z^{\alpha}\bigl(1-Z^{\alpha+1}\bigr)v\,d\vec{x}.

(6.3)

Next, we introduce a new coordinate system
(x,y,z^{\prime})
given by

x=x,y=y,z^{\prime}=\dfrac{1}{\alpha+1}z^{\alpha+1}.

(6.4)

Then
\partial_{z^{\prime}}=z^{-\alpha}\partial_{z}
, and
\int_{\Omega}z^{\alpha}\cdot\,d\vec{x}=\int_{\Omega^{\prime}}\cdot\,dxdydz^{\prime}
where
\Omega^{\prime}=\Omega_{h}\times(0,\frac{1}{\alpha+1})
and
|\Omega^{\prime}|<\infty
. Then we apply the standard Poincaré inequality as follows,

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}z^{\alpha}\bigl|v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}=\int_{\Omega^{\prime}}\bigl|v\bigr|^{2}\,dxdydz^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{\Omega^{\prime}}\bigl|v-\int_{\Omega^{\prime}}v\,dxdydz^{\prime}\bigr|^{2}\,dxdydz^{\prime}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl|\int_{\Omega^{\prime}}v\,dxdydz^{\prime}\bigr|^{2}\lesssim\int_{\Omega^{\prime}}\bigl|\partial_{z^{\prime}}v\bigr|^{2}\,dxdydz^{\prime}+\int_{\Omega^{\prime}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|^{2}\,dxdydz^{\prime}+\bigl|\int_{\Omega}z^{\alpha}v\,d\vec{x}\bigr|^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~=\int_{\Omega}z^{-\alpha}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\int_{\Omega}z^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|^{2}\,dxdydz+\bigl|\int_{\Omega}z^{\alpha}(1-Z^{\alpha+1})v\,d\vec{x}\bigr|^{2}.

Therefore, for
0<\alpha<3
, applying Hardy’s inequality to the right of the above inequality yields,

\displaystyle\int z^{\alpha}\bigl|v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\int z^{-\alpha-\sigma}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\int_{\Omega}z^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|^{2}\,dxdydz+\int z^{2\alpha}\bigl|1-Z^{\alpha+1}\bigr|^{2}\bigl|v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\int z^{2-\alpha-\sigma}\bigl(\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}+\bigl|\partial_{zz}v\bigr|^{2}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}+\int_{\Omega}z^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|^{2}\,dxdydz+\bigl\Arrowvert Z^{\alpha+1}-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\int z^{2\alpha}\bigl|v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\int z^{4-\alpha-\sigma}\bigl(\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}+\bigl|\partial_{zz}v\bigr|^{2}+\bigl|\partial_{zzz}v\bigr|^{2}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+\int_{\Omega}z^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|^{2}\,dxdydz+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert Z^{\alpha+1}-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2},

where
\sigma>0
is a small number such that the following conditions hold

\displaystyle-\alpha-\sigma\neq-1,~2-\alpha-\sigma>-1.

For
0<\alpha<3
, such
\sigma
alway exists.
This finishes the proof.
∎

Now we will show Proposition
14
.

Proof of Proposition
14
.

From (
3.12
), (
3.13
), (
3.17
), (
3.21
), (
3.26
), (
3.28
), (
3.34
), (
3.39
), (
3.43
), there are constants
c_{1},c_{2}\cdots c_{9}
such that the quantities defined by

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}_{d}(t):=\dfrac{1}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{g}{\alpha+2}\int\biggl(\bigl(Z^{\alpha+2}-1\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~-\dfrac{\alpha+2}{\alpha+1}\bigl(Z^{\alpha+1}-1\bigr)\biggr)\,d\vec{x}+c_{2}\int\biggl(\dfrac{\mu}{2}\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|^{2}+\dfrac{\mu+\lambda}{2}\bigl|\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\bigr|^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+\dfrac{\mu}{2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|^{2}\biggr)\,d\vec{x}-\dfrac{c_{2}}{\alpha+1}\int gz^{\alpha}\bigl(Z^{\alpha+1}-1\bigr)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\dfrac{c_{3}}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|v_{t}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{c_{3}g}{2}\int Z^{\alpha}\bigl|Z_{t}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{c_{4}}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\dfrac{c_{4}g}{2}\int Z^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{c_{5}}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{c_{5}g}{2}\int\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\dfrac{c_{7}}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|v_{z}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{c_{9}}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|\nabla_{h}v_{z}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{G}_{d}(t):=\mu/2\int Z\bigl|\nabla v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+c_{1}\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+c_{2}/2\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}\bigl|v_{t}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+c_{3}\mu/2\int Z\bigl|\nabla v_{t}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+c_{4}\mu/2\int Z\bigl|\nabla\nabla_{h}v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+c_{5}\mu/2\int Z\bigl|\nabla\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+c_{6}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+c_{7}\mu/4\int Z\bigl|\nabla v_{z}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+c_{8}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+c_{9}\mu/4\int Z\bigl|\nabla\nabla_{h}v_{z}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x},

satisfy

\dfrac{d}{dt}\mathfrak{E}_{d}(t)+\mathfrak{G}_{d}(t)\leq\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{E}(t))\mathfrak{G}_{d}(t).

Notice that, following similar arguments as in Lemma
1
, one can choose
c_{i}^{\prime}s~(i=1,2\cdots 9)
such that there is a constant
C<\infty
such that

\displaystyle C^{-1}\mathcal{E}(t)\leq\mathfrak{E}_{d}(t)\leq C\mathcal{E}(t),

\displaystyle C^{-1}\mathfrak{D}(t)\leq\mathfrak{G}_{d}(t)\leq C\mathfrak{D}(t).

Moreover, from Lemma
11
, Lemma
3
, and (
2.10
), the above inequality can be extended to

\dfrac{d}{dt}\mathfrak{E}_{d}(t)+\mathfrak{G}_{d}(t)+c_{10}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{E}(t))\mathfrak{G}_{d}(t),

for some positive constant
c_{10}
. Then for
\mathcal{E}(t)
small enough, one has

\dfrac{d}{dt}\mathfrak{E}_{d}(t)+C_{1}\mathfrak{E}_{d}(t)\leq\dfrac{d}{dt}\mathfrak{E}_{d}(t)+\dfrac{1}{2}\mathfrak{G}_{d}(t)+c_{10}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq 0,

for some positive constant
C_{1}<\infty
.
Therefore, one has

\mathfrak{E}_{d}(t)\leq e^{-C_{1}t}\mathfrak{E}_{d}(0),

which finishes the proof.
∎

7
Higher regularity

In this section, we show the regularity of the global solution with more regular initial data. In particular, this shows that the change of coordinates in (
1.6
) is regular, and it justifies the equivalence of (
FBCPE
) and (
1.9
) (or (
1.16
) with the simplified viscosity tensor). Now we write down the equations after differentiating
\eqref{eq:dh-FBCPE}_{1}
,
\eqref{eq:dh-FBCPE}_{3}
horizontally once and
\eqref{eq:dh-FBCPE}_{2}
horizontally twice . It holds,

\begin{cases}z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}\bigl(\partial_{t}v_{hh}+v\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{hh}+W\partial_{z}v_{hh}\bigr)+z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}\bigl(2v_{h}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{h}+v_{hh}\cdot\nabla_{h}v\\
~~~~~~~~+2W_{h}\partial_{z}v_{h}+W_{hh}\partial_{z}v\bigr)+2z^{\alpha}(Z^{\alpha})_{h}\bigl(\partial_{t}v+v\cdot\nabla_{h}v+W\partial_{z}v\bigr)_{h}\\
~~~~~~~~+z^{\alpha}(Z^{\alpha})_{hh}\bigl(\partial_{t}v+v\cdot\nabla_{h}v+W\partial_{z}v\bigr)+g(z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}\nabla_{h}Z)_{hh}\\
~~~~=\mu\Delta_{h}v_{hh}+(\mu+\lambda)\nabla_{h}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{hh}+\mu\partial_{zz}v_{hh}+\bigl(\mu\nabla_{h}\log Z\cdot\nabla_{h}v\\
~~~~~~~~+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\nabla_{h}\log Z\bigr)_{hh},\\
\partial_{t}Z_{hhh}+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{hhh}+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}Z_{hhh}+3(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{hh}\\
~~~~~~~~+3\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}}Z_{hh}+3(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{hh}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{h}+3\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{hh}}Z_{h}\\
~~~~~~~~+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{hhh}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{hhh}}Z=0,\\
z^{\alpha}W_{hh}=z^{\alpha+1}\bigl((\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v}\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{hh}+2(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}\\
~~~~~~~~+(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{hh}}\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z+\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{hh}}\\
~~~~~~~~-(\alpha+1)\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v\,d\xi\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{hh}\\
~~~~~~~~-2(\alpha+1)\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v_{h}\,d\xi\cdot\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}\\
~~~~~~~~-(\alpha+1)\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}v_{hh}\,d\xi\cdot\nabla_{h}\log Z-\int_{0}^{z}\xi^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{hh}\,d\xi.\end{cases}

(7.1)

Proposition 15
.

Suppose in addition to the assumptions as in Proposition
13
,
(Z_{0},v_{0})
satisfies (

\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{0}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{3}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}^{3}v_{0}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}<\infty.

Then for any

, there is a positive constant

such that,

\displaystyle\sup_{0<t<T}\bigl\{\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}^{3}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{3}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr\}+\int_{0}^{T}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla\nabla_{h}^{3}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\,dt

(7.2)

\displaystyle\leq C_{T}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}^{3}v_{0}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{3}Z_{0}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\bigr).

Moreover,

\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{t}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}},\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}<\infty
for any finite
t<\infty
.

Proof.

Taking inner product of
\eqref{eq:dhhh-FBCPE}_{1}
with
-(Z\partial_{hhh}v)_{h}
yields,

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\biggl\{\dfrac{1}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|v_{hhh}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{g}{2}\int\bigl|Z_{hhh}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}\biggr\}

\displaystyle~~~~+\int Z\bigl(\mu\bigl|\nabla_{h}v_{hhh}\bigr|^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bigl|\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{hhh}\bigr|^{2}+\mu\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{hhh}\bigr|^{2}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle=\int\bigl(\mu\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{hh}+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{hh}\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}\bigr)\cdot Zv_{hhh}

\displaystyle~~~~-\bigl(2\mu\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{h}+2(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+\mu\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{hh}\cdot\nabla_{h}v+(\mu+\lambda)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v\nabla_{h}(\log Z)_{hh}\bigr)\cdot(Zv_{hhh})_{h}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-\int z^{\alpha}Z\bigl((Z^{\alpha})_{h}(\partial_{t}v_{hh}+v\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{hh})+Z^{\alpha}v_{h}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{hh}\bigr)\cdot v_{hhh}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-\int z^{\alpha}Z\bigl((Z^{\alpha})_{h}W\partial_{z}v_{hh}+Z^{\alpha}W_{h}\partial_{z}v_{hh}\bigr)\cdot v_{hhh}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\int z^{\alpha}\bigl(Z^{\alpha}(2v_{h}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{h}+v_{hh}\cdot\nabla_{h}v)+2(Z^{\alpha})_{h}(\partial_{t}v+v\cdot\nabla_{h}v)_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+(Z^{\alpha})_{hh}(\partial_{t}v+v\cdot\nabla_{h}v)\bigr)\cdot\bigl(Zv_{hhh}\bigr)_{h}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\int z^{\alpha}\bigl(Z^{\alpha}(2W_{h}\partial_{z}v_{h}+W_{hh}\partial_{z}v)+2(Z^{\alpha})_{h}(W\partial_{z}v)_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+(Z^{\alpha})_{hh}W\partial_{z}v\bigr)\cdot\bigl(Zv_{hhh}\bigr)_{h}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+g\int z^{\alpha}\bigl((Z^{\alpha}-1)Z_{hhh}+2(Z^{\alpha})_{h}Z_{hh}+(Z^{\alpha})_{hh}Z_{h}\bigr)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,(Zv_{hhh})\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-g\int Z_{hhh}\bigl(\dfrac{1-\alpha}{2}\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v}Z_{hhh}+3(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{h}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{hh}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+3\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{h}}Z_{hh}+3(\alpha+1)\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{hh}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+3\overline{z^{\alpha}\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v_{hh}}Z_{h}+\alpha\overline{z^{\alpha}v_{hhh}}\cdot\nabla_{h}Z\bigr)\,d\vec{x}=:\sum_{k=1}^{7}H_{k}.

Now we show the estimates for
H_{k}
’s in the following. Similarly, applying integration by parts and making use of Hölder’s, Minkowski’s, the Sobolev embedding, and Young’s inequalities yield that,

\displaystyle H_{1}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\bigl(\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl|v_{hhh}\bigr|_{4}^{2}+\bigl((\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{8}}^{2})\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}v\bigr|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{3}Z\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{4}}+\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|_{L^{6}}^{3})\bigl|\nabla_{h}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigr)\bigl(\bigl|v_{hhhh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|v_{hhh}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigr)\,dz\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{hhh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{8}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle H_{2}=\dfrac{\alpha}{\alpha+1}\int z^{\alpha}\bigl((Z^{\alpha+1})_{hh}\partial_{t}v_{h}\cdot v_{hhh}+(Z^{\alpha+1})_{h}\partial_{t}v_{h}\cdot v_{hhhh}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-\int z^{\alpha}Z\bigl((Z^{\alpha})_{h}v\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{hh}+Z^{\alpha}v_{h}\cdot\nabla_{h}v_{hh}\bigr)\cdot v_{hhh}\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{hhh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{4}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla\partial_{t}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla\partial_{t}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{4}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{4}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle H_{4}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{hhh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{4}\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+C_{\delta}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{4}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr)+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl\Arrowvert v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla\partial_{t}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle H_{6}\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{hhh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert v_{hhh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert Z-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{4}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle H_{7}\lesssim\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+1\bigr)\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}.

On the other hand,

\displaystyle H_{3}+H_{5}=\int\bigl(Z(Z^{\alpha})_{h}\partial_{z}v_{hh}\cdot v_{hhh}+2(Z^{\alpha})_{h}\partial_{z}v_{h}\cdot(Zv_{hhh})_{h}

\displaystyle~~~~+(Z^{\alpha})_{hh}\partial_{z}v\cdot(Zv_{hhh})_{h}\bigr)z^{\alpha}W\,d\vec{x}+\int\bigl(Z^{\alpha+1}\partial_{z}v_{hh}\cdot v_{hhh}

\displaystyle~~~~+2Z^{\alpha}\partial_{z}v_{h}\cdot(Zv_{hhh})_{h}+2(Z^{\alpha})_{h}\partial_{z}v\cdot(Zv_{hhh})_{h}\bigr)z^{\alpha}W_{h}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\int Z^{\alpha}\partial_{z}v\cdot(Zv_{hhh})_{h}z^{\alpha}W_{hh}\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\bigl(\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\bigl|v_{hhh}\bigr|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}+\bigl|Z_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}+\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}^{2}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}})(\bigl|v_{hhhh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|v_{hhh}\bigr|_{L^{4}})\bigr)\bigl|z^{\alpha}W\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\,dz+\int_{0}^{1}\bigl(\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\bigl|v_{hhh}\bigr|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle~~~~+(\bigl|\partial_{z}v_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}+\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{8}}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{8}})(\bigl|v_{hhhh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|v_{hhh}\bigr|_{L^{4}})\bigr)\bigl|z^{\alpha}W_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\,dz

\displaystyle~~~~+\int_{0}^{1}\bigl|\partial_{z}v\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl(\bigl|v_{hhhh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}+\bigl|Z_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigl|v_{hhh}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\bigr)\bigl|z^{\alpha}W_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\,dz

\displaystyle~~~~\lesssim\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v_{hhh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{hh}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\bigl(\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{E}(t))+1\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}\nabla_{h}^{3}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{3}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\bigr),

where we have applied the following facts

\displaystyle\bigl|z^{\alpha}W\bigr|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{3}_{h}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}},

\displaystyle\bigl|z^{\alpha}W_{h}\bigr|_{L^{4}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{3}_{h}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}\bigr),

\displaystyle\bigl|z^{\alpha}W_{hh}\bigr|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{3}_{h}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{3}\bigr).

Summing up the above inequalities with a small enough
\delta>0
yields,

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\biggl\{\dfrac{1}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{3}v\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{g}{2}\int\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{3}Z\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}\biggr\}

\displaystyle~~~~+\mu/2\int Z\bigl(\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{4}v\bigr|^{2}+\bigl|\partial_{z}\nabla_{h}^{3}v\bigr|^{2}\bigr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl(\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{E}(t))+1\bigr)\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla v_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~\times\bigl(\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}Z^{\alpha+1}\nabla_{h}^{3}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{3}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+1\bigr).

Then applying the Grönwall’s inequality to above inequality will show (
7.2
). On the other hand, notice (
1.17
). One has

\bigl\Arrowvert\partial_{t}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{\infty}}\leq\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{3}_{h}}.

This finishes the proof.
∎

8
Global stability theory of a non-conservative system

System (
1.16
), as discussed in Remark
2
, satisfies the conservations of mass, momentum, and energy; see Lemma
4
. To provide a foundation for application, e.g. simulation etc., we introduce the following non-conservative system with constant viscosity:

\begin{cases}(\alpha+1)z(\partial_{t}Z+v\cdot\nabla_{h}Z)+zZ(\mathrm{div}_{h}\,v+\partial_{z}W)\\
~~~~~~~~+\alpha ZW=0&\text{in}~\Omega,\\
\rho(\partial_{t}v+v\cdot\nabla_{h}v+W\partial_{z}v+g\nabla_{h}Z)=\Delta_{h}v+\partial_{zz}v&\text{in}~\Omega,\\
\partial_{z}Z=0&\text{in}~\Omega,\end{cases}

(8.1)

with the boundary condition (
1.20
). The same identities (
1.17
) and (
1.18
) still hold. For the initial data, we assume again

\int Z^{\alpha+1}\,d\vec{x}|_{t=0}=\int Z_{0}^{\alpha+1}\,d\vec{x}=1.

(8.2)

Then one can check, for the non-conservative system (
8.1
), the conservation of mass still holds, i.e.,

\int Z^{\alpha+1}\,d\vec{x}=\int Z_{0}^{\alpha+1}\,d\vec{x}=1

(8.3)

for a solution.

Then one can easily check the calculation, and conclude that, the same global stability theory holds for solutions
(v,Z)
to (
8.1
). That is

Theorem 3

(Global Stability for the Non-conservative System)
.

Under the same assumptions as in Theorem
1
, there exists a unique global solution
(v,Z)
to system (
8.1
), which satisfies the same regularity and estimates as in Theorem
1
.

On the other hand, to describe the asymptotic stability, let

\delta(t):=\dfrac{\int\rho vZ\,d\vec{x}}{\int\rho Z\,d\vec{x}}=(\alpha+1)\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}v\,d\vec{x}.

(8.4)

We remind readers that we have assumed
A_{\alpha}=1
for the sake of clear presentation.
Then from
\eqref{FB-CPE-non-conservative}_{2}
, one can verify

\dfrac{d}{dt}\delta(t)=\delta^{\prime}(t)=-(\alpha+1)\int(\nabla_{h}Z\cdot\nabla_{h})v\,d\vec{x}\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}v\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}.

(8.5)

Then from Theorem
3
, we have that there exists
\delta_{0}>0
such that

\lim_{t\rightarrow\infty}\delta(t)=\delta_{\infty}.

(8.6)

Moreover, the energy functional for the non-conservative system is defined by replacing
v
with
v-\delta(t)
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{n}(t):=\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}(v-\delta(t))\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}_{h}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}(v-\delta(t))_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}

(8.7)

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}(v-\delta(t))_{z}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}_{h}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert(v-\delta(t))\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\bigl\Arrowvert Z-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}^{2}+\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2},

with the initial energy
\mathcal{E}_{n,0}
defined similarly.

Rewrite
\eqref{FB-CPE-non-conservative}_{2}
as

\displaystyle z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}(\partial_{t}(v-\delta(t))+v\cdot\nabla_{h}(v-\delta(t))+W\partial_{z}(v-\delta(t))+g\nabla_{h}Z)

(8.8)

\displaystyle=\Delta_{h}(v-\delta(t))+\partial_{zz}(v-\delta(t))-z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}\delta^{\prime}(t).

Then taking the
L^{2}
-inner product of (
8.5
) with
Z(v-\delta(t))
and following the same calculation as in Lemma
4
lead to

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dt}\biggl\{\dfrac{1}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}|v-\delta(t)|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{g}{\alpha+2}\int(Z^{\alpha+2}-1)-\dfrac{\alpha+2}{\alpha+1}(Z^{\alpha+1}-1)\,d\vec{x}\biggr\}

(8.9)

\displaystyle+\int Z(|\nabla_{h}(v-\delta(t))|^{2}+|\partial_{z}(v-\delta(t))|^{2})\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle=-\int\nabla_{h}Z\cdot\nabla_{h}(v-\delta(t))\cdot(v-\delta(t))\,d\vec{x}-\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\delta^{\prime}(t)(v-\delta(t))\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle\leq\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{6}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}(v-\delta(t))\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{3}}\bigl\Arrowvert v-\delta(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl\Arrowvert v-\delta(t)\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}(v-\delta(t))\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{1}}.

Then from (
8.9
), (
3.13
) (
3.17
), (
3.21
), (
3.26
), (
3.28
), (
3.34
), (
3.39
), (
3.43
), there exist constants
d_{1},d_{2}\cdots d_{9}
such that the quantities defined by

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}_{n,d}(t):=\dfrac{1}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|v-\delta(t)\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{g}{\alpha+2}\int\biggl(\bigl(Z^{\alpha+2}-1\bigr)

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~-\dfrac{\alpha+2}{\alpha+1}\bigl(Z^{\alpha+1}-1\bigr)\biggr)\,d\vec{x}+d_{2}\int\biggl(\dfrac{1}{2}\bigl|\nabla_{h}(v-\delta(t))\bigr|^{2}+\dfrac{1}{2}\bigl|\partial_{z}(v-\delta(t))\bigr|^{2}\biggr)\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~-\dfrac{d_{2}}{\alpha+1}\int gz^{\alpha}\bigl(Z^{\alpha+1}-1\bigr)\mathrm{div}_{h}\,(v-\delta(t))\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\dfrac{d_{3}}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|(v-\delta(t))_{t}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{d_{3}g}{2}\int Z^{\alpha}\bigl|Z_{t}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\dfrac{d_{4}}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|\nabla_{h}(v-\delta(t))\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{d_{4}g}{2}\int Z^{\alpha}\bigl|\nabla_{h}Z\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\dfrac{d_{5}}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}(v-\delta(t))\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{d_{5}g}{2}\int\bigl|\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\dfrac{d_{7}}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|(v-\delta(t))_{z}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{d_{9}}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha+1}\bigl|\nabla_{h}(v-\delta(t))_{z}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{G}_{n,d}(t):=\dfrac{1}{2}\int Z\bigl|\nabla(v-\delta(t))\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+d_{1}\bigl\Arrowvert Z_{t}\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\dfrac{d_{2}}{2}\int z^{\alpha}Z^{\alpha}\bigl|(v-\delta(t))_{t}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\dfrac{d_{3}}{2}\int Z\bigl|\nabla(v-\delta(t))_{t}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+\dfrac{d_{4}}{2}\int Z\bigl|\nabla\nabla_{h}(v-\delta(t))\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+\dfrac{d_{5}}{2}\int Z\bigl|\nabla\nabla_{h}^{2}(v-\delta(t))\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}+d_{6}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\dfrac{d_{7}}{4}\int Z\bigl|\nabla v_{z}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x}

\displaystyle~~~~+d_{8}\bigl\Arrowvert\nabla_{h}^{2}Z\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}+\dfrac{d_{9}}{4}\int Z\bigl|\nabla\nabla_{h}(v-\delta(t))_{z}\bigr|^{2}\,d\vec{x},

satisfy

\dfrac{d}{dt}\mathfrak{E}_{n,d}(t)+\mathfrak{G}_{n,d}(t)\leq\mathcal{H}(\mathcal{E}_{n}(t))\mathfrak{G}_{n,d}(t).

Then similar arguments as in Lemma
1
, one can choose
d_{i}
’s(
i=1,2\cdots 9
) such that there is a constant
C\in(0,\infty)
such that

C^{-1}\mathcal{E}_{n}(t)\leq\mathfrak{E}_{n,d}(t)\leq C\mathcal{E}_{n}(t),

and similar Poincaré inequality in
Lemma
11
and Lemma
3
hold for
v
replaced by
v-\delta(t)
. Therefore, for
\mathcal{E}_{n}(t)
small enough, we arrive at

\begin{gathered}\dfrac{d}{dt}\mathfrak{E}_{n,d}(t)+C_{n,1}\mathfrak{E}_{d}(t)\leq\dfrac{d}{dt}\mathfrak{E}_{n,d}(t)+\dfrac{1}{2}\mathfrak{G}_{n,d}(t)\\
+d_{10}\bigl\Arrowvert z^{\alpha/2}(v-\delta(t))\bigr\Arrowvert_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq 0,\end{gathered}

(8.10)

for some positive constants
d_{10}
and
C_{n,1}<\infty
. Therefore, one has that

\mathcal{E}_{n}(t)\leq C\mathfrak{E}_{n,d}(t)\leq Ce^{-C_{n,1}t}\mathfrak{E}_{n,d}(0).

(8.11)

Moreover, from (
8.8
), one has that

|\delta^{\prime}(t)|\leq\mathcal{E}_{n}(t)\leq Ce^{-C_{n,1}t}\mathfrak{E}_{n,d}(0).

(8.12)

Consequently, it follows that

|\delta(t)-\delta_{\infty}|=|\int_{t}^{\infty}\delta^{\prime}(s)\,ds|\leq\dfrac{C}{C_{n,1}}e^{-C_{n,1}t}\mathfrak{E}_{n,d}(0).

(8.13)

Moreover, Proposition
10
and, in particular (
4.1
), still hold with
v
and
\mathcal{E}(t)
replaced by
v-\delta(t)
and
\mathcal{E}_{n}(t)
, respectively.

Therefore, we have proved the following theorem:

Theorem 4

(Asymptotic Stability for the Non-conservative System)
.

Suppose that in addition to the conditions given in Theorem
3
,
0<\alpha<3
(or equivalently
\gamma>\frac{4}{3}
).
Then there are constants
\varepsilon^{\prime\prime\prime}\in(0,1)
small enough and
C_{n,1},C_{n,2}\in(0,\infty)
such that if
\mathcal{E}_{n,0}<\varepsilon^{\prime\prime\prime}
, we have

\mathcal{E}_{n}(t)\leq e^{-C_{n,1}}C_{n,2}\mathcal{E}_{n,0}.

(8.14)

Moreover, there exists
\delta_{\infty}
as defined in (
8.6
) such that

|Z-1|+|v-\delta_{\infty}|\leq\bigl\Arrowvert Z-1\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}+\bigl\Arrowvert v-\delta_{\infty}\bigr\Arrowvert_{H^{2}}\leq C_{n}e^{-C_{n}t},

(8.15)

for some constant
C_{n}\in(0,\infty)
.
